## Mapping `m_CPM_AFPS_0600_Crossfoot_Message_Details`

- Target instances: `CPM_AFPS_CROSSFOOT_FILE`

### Transformation `SQ_ERROR_TBL` (Source Qualifier)

- **Sql Query**: `SELECT a.ERROR_MESSAGE, a.SOURCE_KEY, a.ERROR_DATE, a.PP_END_YEAR, a.PP_NUM, a.CYCLE_ID, a.ERROR_CODE FROM ERROR_TBL a, (SELECT b1.PP_END_YEAR, b1.PP_NUM, MAX(b1.ERROR_DATE) as ERROR_DATE, MAX(b1.CYCLE_ID) as CYCLE_ID FROM ERROR_TBL b1 WHERE b1.ERROR_DATE >= TRUNC(SYSDATE) AND b1.PP_END_YEAR = TO_NUMBER('$$MAP_PP_END_YEAR') AND b1.PP_NUM = TO_NUMBER('$$MAP_PP_NUM') AND b1.CYCLE_ID = $$MAP_CYCLE_ID AND b1.PROCESS_NAME = 'm_CPM_AFPS_0400_Crossfoot_Errors' GROUP BY b1.PP_END_YEAR, b1.PP_NUM) b WHERE a.ERROR_DATE = b.ERROR_DATE AND a.PP_END_YEAR = TO_NUMBER('$$MAP_PP_END_YEAR') AND a.PP_NUM = TO_NUMBER('$$MAP_PP_NUM') AND a.CYCLE_ID = b.CYCLE_ID AND a.PROCESS_NAME = 'm_CPM_AFPS_0400_Crossfoot_Errors' ORDER BY a.SOURCE_KEY`
- **Number Of Sorted Ports**: `0`
- **Select Distinct**: `NO`

- SQL override `SQ_ERROR_TBL` positional bindings: 1 → `PROCESS_NAME` = `a.ERROR_MESSAGE`; 2 → `ERROR_MESSAGE` = `a.SOURCE_KEY`; 3 → `SOURCE_KEY` = `a.ERROR_DATE`; 4 → `ERROR_DATE` = `a.PP_END_YEAR`; 5 → `PP_END_YEAR` = `a.PP_NUM`; 6 → `PP_NUM` = `a.CYCLE_ID`; 7 → `CYCLE_ID` = `a.ERROR_CODE`

|---|---|---|---|
| CPM_AFPS_CROSSFOOT_FILE | SUBJECT | exp_Final_Message.SUBJECT → exp_Convert.o_ERROR_MESSAGE = REPLACESTR(0, ERROR_MESSAGE, EMPL_ID, v_MASK_EMPLID) → exp_Initial.ERROR_MESSAGE → SQ_ERROR_TBL.ERROR_MESSAGE → ERROR_TBL.ERROR_MESSAGE → exp_Initial.EMPL_ID → SQ_ERROR_TBL.SOURCE_KEY → ERROR_TBL.SOURCE_KEY → exp_Convert.v_MASK_EMPLID = 'XXX-XX-' \|\| SUBSTR(EMPL_ID, 6, 4) | ERROR_TBL.ERROR_MESSAGE; ERROR_TBL.SOURCE_KEY |
| CPM_AFPS_CROSSFOOT_FILE | MESSAGE | exp_Final_Message.MESSAGE → exp_Convert.ERROR_CODE → exp_Initial.ERROR_CODE → SQ_ERROR_TBL.ERROR_CODE → ERROR_TBL.ERROR_CODE | ERROR_TBL.ERROR_CODE |
